# Third-party notices

This file lists the third-party open-source packages in the production dependency tree of this repository (the game server, the web app and the workspace packages they use), with their licences and the licence and notice texts they ship. Our own code is proprietary (UNLICENSED) and is not listed.

Generated by `node scripts/generate-third-party-notices.mjs` from `package-lock.json` (entries that are not dev-only), cross-checked with `npm ls --omit=dev --all --json`. Do not edit by hand; re-run the script after any dependency change.

- Lockfile SHA-256: `cd2f9c5a8d0941924ff82d0e459abc7a12b6182788b8ef10d52489eb6be5a675`
- Packages: 218 (53 optional, 7 not installed where this file was generated, so listed with the lockfile's licence only)

## Licences with obligations beyond keeping this notice

### LGPL-3.0-or-later: libvips (bundled by @img/sharp-*; sharp is an optional dependency of Next.js)

Packages: [`@img/sharp-win32-x64@0.35.4`](#img-sharp-win32-x64-0-35-4) (Apache-2.0 AND LGPL-3.0-or-later, win32/x64)

Also declared by sharp for other platforms but missing from package-lock.json (they WILL be in a Linux image once the lockfile lists them): `@img/sharp-libvips-darwin-arm64@1.3.3`, `@img/sharp-libvips-darwin-x64@1.3.3`, `@img/sharp-libvips-linux-arm@1.3.3`, `@img/sharp-libvips-linux-arm64@1.3.3`, `@img/sharp-libvips-linux-ppc64@1.3.3`, `@img/sharp-libvips-linux-riscv64@1.3.3`, `@img/sharp-libvips-linux-s390x@1.3.3`, `@img/sharp-libvips-linux-x64@1.3.3`, `@img/sharp-libvips-linuxmusl-arm64@1.3.3`, `@img/sharp-libvips-linuxmusl-x64@1.3.3`. Same obligations.

The sharp platform packages ship a prebuilt shared libvips library (and its dependencies) under the GNU Lesser General Public License v3.0 or later. sharp's own JavaScript is Apache-2.0.
Obligations when the web image (or any distributed build) contains them:
- Keep this notice and the LGPL-3.0 and GPL-3.0 licence texts with the distribution (the package's LICENSE file reproduced below; the LGPL text is at https://www.gnu.org/licenses/lgpl-3.0.txt).
- Keep libvips as a separately replaceable shared library (it is: the .so/.dll files inside the @img/sharp-* package); do not statically link it into other code and do not prohibit users from replacing it or reverse engineering for debugging such modifications.
- Make the corresponding libvips source available for the exact version shipped (upstream: https://github.com/libvips/libvips and the build recipe at https://github.com/lovell/sharp-libvips), e.g. by a written offer or by hosting the source next to the image.
- If image optimisation is not needed, Next.js can run without sharp (images.unoptimized, or do not install optional dependencies in the image), which removes this obligation.

### CC-BY-4.0: caniuse-lite (browser support data used by browserslist / Next.js)

Packages: [`caniuse-lite@1.0.30001810`](#caniuse-lite-1-0-30001810) (CC-BY-4.0)

The data is from caniuse.com by Alexis Deveria, licensed under Creative Commons Attribution 4.0 International (https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by/4.0/).
Obligations: keep this attribution (creator, the licence name and link, and a link to the source https://github.com/browserslist/caniuse-lite), and state if the data was modified (it is not). The data is used at build time; little or none of it ships to browsers, but the attribution is kept wherever the package is redistributed (for example inside the web image).
